- **outer-loop**: Bilevel loop engineering — put a loop on top of a loop. When a recurring process already runs (a sweep, a pipeline, a review cycle) but keeps getting stuck the same way, this reads its traces, finds where the SEARCH is stuck rather than where the output is wrong, and proposes a change to the mechanism itself. Based on Karpathy's AutoResearch loop plus Qu & Lu's "Bilevel Autoresearch" (arXiv 2603.23420), which got a verified 5x over a single loop using the SAME model at both levels — the gain is architectural, not intelligence.
